### Exact-vacuous extension ground fails with a retained consumer

This investigation starts at main `adcc956f87740a7b456b20428099a569f0b8426c`, tree `d83f53ac1721613bea2a1fdf65a71cedb9bcea2c`.
It tests a proposed sufficient semantic ground before changing any inference rule: a pure extension chain U→B→A, with exact unchanged effective content/AUs and independently qualified non-RE01 premises, need not have a complete B-v1 U construction.
The following fixed parent has no complete U child under arbitrary permitted finite additions; this is stronger than rejecting one supplied certificate.

#### Judgments and exact source

Write Q_B(Γ,P,T,A) for the accepted semantic clause-1.5 judgment in immutable actual Γ and exact parent P, independently of finite checker evidence.
A permitted finite candidate must supply the two derivation steps and correspondence and satisfy every applicable predicate of its required roots and consumed components in that candidate.
Full component validity V includes any applicable Q_B duty; qualifying local premises with that duty still pending does not establish V.

The [original source](../test/conformance/fixtures/xsd/recovery/exact-vacuous-consumer.xsd) gives A the exact raw unit sequence `[H0..1]`.
Original nonabstract global H has exact named complex type B, empty final and block containing restriction; B vacuously extends A, R restricts A to empty and X extends R to empty.
The base chains are acyclic, while H:B and B/A content references form legal component-reference recursion; A is emptiable and its language is nonempty.

All original local/source predicates qualify, and a complete one-node original-X certificate uses existing B without additions.
This independently establishes original obligations without assuming the pending helper judgment below.
All relevant type flags and AU identities are empty, and no type has an attribute wildcard.

#### Locally qualified parent with exact shared content

The finite parent P replaces X by a restriction of existing B and adds U, M, R_H, R_M and four distinct consumer particles.
U extends exact B vacuously and has the literal original A content particle; M is nonabstract, has exact declared type U and affiliates directly with H.
R_H restricts B with unit sequence `[H1]`, while R_M restricts R_H with unit sequence `[M1]`; their roots and required use particles are distinct fresh identities.

Every original and fresh parent component passes its local construction predicates with no residual non-RE01 obligation; U is the sole nonprimitive duty and B has its existing direct primitive discharge.
The original content owner derives B's effective content from exact A, and raw construction and candidate correspondence qualify the supplied properties.
Thus A, B and U share one exact raw content component, empty effective AUs, absent wildcards, nonabstract status and empty final/block sets; this is stronger than textual or property-copy equality.

Parent effective(H) contains H and M, so R_M's required M maps to the same global M leaf in R_H's implicit choice.
R_H's required H use narrows B's optional H use, and all attribution, QName, occurrence, declaration and type predicates qualify.
These are local premises, not an assumption that the parent's pending U judgment or complete V already holds.

#### No complete U child under arbitrary finite additions

Any permitted nonprimitive U child retains exact U and replaces its edge by restriction of an intermediate I that immediately extends exact A.
Any successful U-to-B derivation contains that first restriction edge; missing exact B instead fails M's required affiliation legality.
By [Type Derivation OK](https://www.w3.org/TR/2004/REC-xmlschema-1-20041028/#cos-ct-derived-ok) and [Substitution Group OK](https://www.w3.org/TR/2004/REC-xmlschema-1-20041028/#cos-equiv-derived-ok-rec), H's restriction block therefore excludes retained M from effective(H), independently of I and every further finite addition.

Retained nonabstract M remains its own mandatory leaf in effective(M).
Every effective(H) alternative is global and none may acquire M's reserved expanded QName under [global uniqueness](https://www.w3.org/TR/2004/REC-xmlschema-1-20041028/#sch-props-correct); new affiliations under either head cannot remove M or restore its blocked membership.
The identity and complex-type premises exclude replacing U/B by a content-equal component or using simple-type alternatives to erase the first restriction.

Restriction normalization removes the pointless unit singleton wrappers but retains the distinct H/M uses or their implicit choices.
Two elements fail [NameAndTypeOK.1](https://www.w3.org/TR/2004/REC-xmlschema-1-20041028/#rcase-NameAndTypeOK); element-to-choice uses RecurseAsIfGroup/RecurseLax and lacks an M target; choice-to-choice must map every derived leaf, including M.
Choice-to-element has no permitted dispatch unless a singleton normalizes back to the same QName failure.

Neither consumer has a wildcard, and their distinct roots/uses prevent a same-particle shortcut.
Added helper content cannot rewrite the inherited consumer particles, substitute a duplicate global QName or turn an element's declared type content into a consumer alternative.
Thus R_M necessarily fails [derivation-ok-restriction.5.4](https://www.w3.org/TR/2004/REC-xmlschema-1-20041028/#derivation-ok-restriction) in every proposed child.

R_H, R_M and all their particles remain fresh relative to immutable Γ and mandatory roots in every descendant.
This local contradiction precedes any nested RE01 evidence, so no complete U child exists, independently of finite-evidence adequacy or a fixed-point interpretation.
It refutes the general implication from exact-vacuous extension properties and qualified local premises to Q_B, while original X still has its separate complete positive without this trapped helper inventory.

#### Complete contrast and authority limits

The [typed controls](../test/research/re01/exact-vacuous-ground.test.ts) qualify all original and parent local premises, the source-derived exact A/B content, U's raw extension and both candidate correspondences.
They preserve a complete original-X positive, check the same-context R_M/R_H comparison and distinguish legal affiliation from restriction-blocked effective membership before verifying the complete child failure.
The universal quantifier follows from the first-edge/QName proof, not from this one executed child.

An independent positive changes only M's declared type to exact B when creating the parent, retaining the same identities and complete root inventory.
U still has the same nonprimitive vacuous chain, but rebasing U preserves M's membership; the complete two-node certificate passes with every inherited fresh root checked.
The [independent source/reference controls](../test/conformance/reference/re01-exact-vacuous-ground.test.ts) pin original types, flags, ranges and source contributions, and record source/recursive-instance acceptance separately from full RE01 semantics.

The pinned dated XML SHA-256 remains `e496af408b14853e6169ac7c1fca09d55a81bd73771758ad6be020960e1317ba`.
As already recorded in the [conditional rank discussion](#r4-retained-obligations-and-a-conditional-well-founded-measure), the cos-ct-extends.1.5 note says the all-extension case is “trivial to check”, not unconditionally satisfied; its construction recipe remains conditional on a valid restriction.
The accepted direct same-component epsilon ground is preserved, and no new ground, recognizer extension, production negative, authority version or failure category is introduced.

#### Surviving renewal-specific proof attempt

The general counterexample does not refute a theorem restricted to the preceding renewal family Cᵢ.
That family's retained X requires some H-effective/Q-ineffective supporter rather than the particular Mᵢ: fresh Uᵢ₊₁/Mᵢ₊₁ can replace lost support and restore every local predicate.
The fixed R_M consumer here requires exact M, which cannot be replaced by a different QName.

For the renewal family, let qᵢ abbreviate Q_B(Γ,Cᵢ,Uᵢ,A), and keep q_X for the separate actual X judgment.
Complete local qualification of Cᵢ₊₁ proves qᵢ₊₁ implies qᵢ for every natural i; the root construction proves q₀ implies q_X.
These conditional rules include all inherited roots, consumed candidate X and affiliations; original X(Γ) is not inserted as a repeated child debt.

The attempted finite symbolic proof packages the uniform local invariant and successor witness function i↦Cᵢ₊₁, then tries to conclude q₀.
Its first unsupported inference is from all those local facts and implications qᵢ₊₁⇒qᵢ to q₀: assigning every qᵢ false satisfies the displayed premises.
This is only a countervaluation to that proposed inference, not a model of all accepted B semantics or a claim of policy ambiguity.

Ordinary induction has no established base q value, and the known implication points from successor to predecessor.
Finite acyclic type ancestry and [recursive schema-property closure](https://www.w3.org/TR/2004/REC-xmlschema-1-20041028/#conformance-schemaValidity) concern one finite context; Cᵢ₊₁ is a generated witness context, not a property-valued component of Cᵢ.
Likewise the two-step clause bounds each derivation path without supplying an inference across arbitrarily many qualification contexts.

The remaining concrete proposition is a semantic soundness theorem for a finite symbolic guarded proof of this exact family, including complete reconstruction of every finite context and inherited obligation.
Its proof must derive the simultaneous-validity introduction from independent dated semantics; assuming qᵢ₊₁, invoking the same unproved ground recursively or merely restating postfixed local closure cannot establish it.
A representative quotient needs sound extraction/reconstruction as well, and a quotient cycle alone supplies no base judgment.

This investigation proves neither renewal-specific semantic success nor semantic nonexistence, general evidence inadequacy or impossibility of a complete procedure.
General coverage, input-derived bounds or an equivalent terminating negative criterion, mathematical termination and full #234/#232 acceptance remain open.
All seven recovery packages, B-v1, finite-evidence-v1, AU C1/PW R-240/DT A/#178/GSC1/P1/F2, original operands, configurable sticky budgets and later #179/#153/#184 ownership remain unchanged.

### Global formal models and renewal nonentailment

This proof continuation starts at main `df0aa13e38eb7352344ce4f1e2f21a4fb3050761`, tree `26e95d332f86e8038e641e9cca59a0b9f3cc4f0b`.

#### Exact judgments and the two validity sorts

Fix any admitted immutable actual Γ, without assuming its pending RE01 obligations.
Let J contain every applicable contextual own clause-1.5 judgment `(Γ,P,T,A)`, including direct primitive judgments, over all permitted finite coherent parents P, with exact target T, designated ancestor A, component maps, ordered incidence, source/QName bindings, original typed operands and generated-slot bindings.
The disjoint union over admitted Γ gives the full judgment universe; it is a set of finite encoded records, not an infinite candidate object or a set of runtime allocation events.

The [selected contextual contract](#quantified-claim-and-candidate-families) applies B-v1 uniformly to every consumed clause-1.5 obligation, including fresh helpers and inherited children.
The theorem assumes that uniform interpretation, rather than root-only B followed by an independent opaque Q_raw for nested checks.
This scopes the formalization to the selected project contract; it does not assert that unmodified XSD prose mandates that selection.

Opaque context IDs, caches, evidence storage labels and engineering budgets are absent from these semantic records.
Duplicate handles with the same exact maps and bindings present one mathematical context; this neither renames component identities nor deletes or merges inherited helpers.
All original/source domains, scalar contexts and negative lookups remain explicit data.

Q(j) denotes the bare own clause-1.5 property; V(H,R) denotes full validity of roots R and their consumed component closure in exact H.
The accepted direct epsilon ground establishes a primitive component's own clause only after its other required premises; it does not assign that clause unconditional truth when a consumed duty fails.
Simple-base extension clause 2 has no clause-1.5 duty.

Complete actual assessment is separate: original source/local qualification in Γ conjoined with every original Q obligation, including primitive ones.
Original X(Γ) is not assumed initially or inserted into each candidate merely because it is available.
A genuine original-operand dependency retains its exact owning context rather than borrowing validity into H.

#### Complete predicate and polarity audit

The proof uses the [accepted complete local inventory](content-model-s06-re-01-resolver-implementation.md#complete-268-rule-inventory) and aggregate particle/construction owners, including all selected AU/scalar/source rules.
It does not infer local completeness from implementation receipts, finite-verifier coverage flags or code discovery alone.
Missing authentic properties, metadata and accepted external prerequisites remain input qualifications, not false local predicates or negative Q facts.

The pinned dated Structures XML SHA-256 remains `e496af408b14853e6169ac7c1fca09d55a81bd73771758ad6be020960e1317ba`.
The following audit identifies all relevant ways the formalized schema-construction constraints can consume Q.

| Accepted predicate family | Primary or accepted authority | Dependence on Q |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Complex extension 1.5 | `cos-ct-extends`, pinned lines 4070–4084 | Positive own clause |
| Complex derivation relation | `cos-ct-derived-ok`, 4277 onward | Raw identity, bases, methods and exclusions only |

The `cd-ct-extension` term is defined at pinned line 4106 but has no downstream term-reference use importing clause 1.5 into another relation.
In particular, “validly derived” in declaration and particle clauses explicitly invokes the raw complex/simple derivation relations, not full V or a recursive RE01 search.
Negative membership/lookups and first-success scalar tests are Q-free; an invalid component cannot be skipped as a false Q alternative.

Complete declared affiliation enumeration precedes effective filtering, so blocked or abstract members retain their positive legality duties.
Structures 5.1 requires recursive property-value component constraints plus source/schema-representation conditions and identifies these as the defined schema-construction error kinds.
Legal property-reference cycles close one finite component conjunction; a generated successor context is not such a property edge.

#### Local choices and exact finite frontiers

For a finite geometric candidate c, let ℓ record its semantic local choices, including coherent implicit-group enumerations, particle mappings and permitted AU alternatives.
Candidate geometry and branch guards depend on exact records rather than Q truth; ℓ is not a finite RE01 proof.
Q-free scalar conditions retain their complete mathematical value/alias quantifiers without enumerating them as Q obligations.

L(c,ℓ) asserts every Q-free correspondence, source and local premise.
F(c,ℓ) is exactly the finite set of all own clause-1.5 obligations in the complete required closure, including primitive ones, with each subject and context retained.
The roots are E, the coherent endpoint and every fresh component relative to immutable Γ, including inherited additions; unrelated original availability does not create hypothetical validity duties.

The local plan parameter avoids replacing existentially valid alternatives by an unjustified larger dependency inventory.
Failed exploratory branches do not add semantic duties unless those components are already mandatory roots or property/predicate dependencies.
All mandatory incoming declarations and original typed operands remain in the appropriate closure regardless of the chosen mapping.

For any interpretation S of all own-Q variables, full validity factors exactly as follows:

```text
V_S(c) ⇔ ∃ℓ. L(c,ℓ) ∧ F(c,ℓ) ⊆ S
```

Necessity extracts the choices and every required clause of a valid candidate.
Sufficiency checks every Q-free condition by L and every own clause by S membership, thereby satisfying the entire finite required closure.
No full V or pending Q is assumed to justify a local branch, and no primitive obligation is erased from F.

#### Global operator and two-way model reconstruction

Let Cand(j) contain every permitted finite B-v1 geometric candidate for j before full validity is assumed.
It imposes no helper cap, source-only restriction or chosen renewal recipe.
The accepted primitive family also permits the exact same-component epsilon candidate: its full closure retains its own Q and every other required duty.
It changes no context, component or derivation edge, and every inherited fresh root remains required.
Define N on subsets of J:

```text
N(S) = { j ∈ J | ∃c ∈ Cand(j), ∃ℓ. L(c,ℓ) ∧ F(c,ℓ) ⊆ S }
```

The stated semantic-success definition means a permitted finite candidate supplies the two derivation steps, correspondence and every applicable required predicate.
Applying that definition and both directions of the full-validity factorization gives the global candidate equation Q=N(Q).
This is an operator on exact judgments, distinct from the earlier operator on supplied construction nodes.

N is monotone because eligibility, branch conditions, L and F are Q-independent and all Q occurrences are positive.
It is finitary because each chosen finite candidate and plan has a finite frontier, even though the complete candidate universe can be infinite.
Exact record-preserving presentation changes leave candidates, L and F invariant; if duplicate presentation labels are retained, their permutation commutes with N.

For any fixed point S, interpret every own Q by membership in S and V by the finite-closure factorization.
This faithfully reconstructs the defining equations and truth values of every translated component/source predicate and the actual-assessment conjunction, including all inherited roots and exact Γ contexts.
It does not make every candidate or original schema valid: a candidate may have V false, and the original assessment result is not an initial premise.

For j outside S there is no candidate with the required V under that interpretation, since S=N(S); conversely every model of these candidate equations restricts to a fixed point of N.
This correspondence alone does not show that every fixed point obeys the additional accepted conditional primitive rules or finite-positive results.
For example, mutually referring direct primitive components can have finite valid component closure even though neither can be introduced first by a rule demanding the other's prior own-Q truth.

The two models constructed next satisfy those additional accepted rules as well, without adding a simultaneous primitive ground or claiming that the least fixed point of N preserves all accepted positives.
This proof does not claim to formalize every runtime/conformance behavior or every possible independent reading of the normative phrase “possible to derive”.
A proposed omitted condition must identify a concrete accepted predicate or independently justified derivability principle; unspecified additional meaning is not a failed occurrence or inventory proof.

The [primitive approval](https://github.com/TechSpokes/typescript-wsdl-client/issues/234#issuecomment-6112070199) is a sufficient truth rule that excludes some candidate-equation fixed points; it establishes its own clause after its other premises, rather than full validity without those duties.
The [accepted construction/evidence checkpoint](https://github.com/TechSpokes/typescript-wsdl-client/issues/266#issuecomment-6143144936) preserves finite-evidence-v1 while leaving semantic evidence adequacy to #234.
Rejecting cyclic storage evidence therefore does not assert that every semantic success has a finite proof; the [semantic-success definition](#semantic-success-and-the-adequacy-question) distinguishes a finite environment from such proof.
Selected F2 is a Q-free scalar predicate; the proof below shows that both specific models satisfy the primitive and finite-positive rules, and these accepted choices do not distinguish between them.

#### A hereditary exclusion set and two greatest models

The fixed-point existence argument is the classical [Knaster–Tarski theorem](https://msp.org/pjm/1955/5-2/pjm-v5-n2-p11-s.pdf).
Let Jₙ be the nonprimitive subset of J and define an auxiliary optimistic projection:

```text
F_gen(c,ℓ) = { d ∈ F(c,ℓ) ∩ Jₙ | P_d ≠ Γ_d }
R(A) = { j ∈ Jₙ | ∃c ∈ Cand(j), ∃ℓ.
                 L(c,ℓ) ∧ F_gen(c,ℓ) ⊆ A }
D = Jₙ \ μR
K = J \ D
```

Here P_d is d's exact owning context and Γ_d its immutable actual context.
Only the auxiliary premise frontier drops primitive duties and duties owned by actual Γ; R's domain still contains every nonprimitive target, including actual Γ targets.
No actual obligation becomes automatically true: each conclusion still needs its own candidate and all retained generated premises.

This optimism is neither a semantic interpretation nor a positive test.
Full N, V, the postfixed supports and actual assessment retain every original-context duty unchanged.
R's finite frontiers make μR the union of finite iterations, equivalently finite well-founded derivability for the projected rules, without any implementation-coverage claim.

Every locally qualified candidate for j in D has a retained generated nonprimitive frontier member in D.
Otherwise all its finitely many F_gen premises would have finite R derivations; grafting those derivations beneath the candidate would derive j, contradicting j outside μR.
Thus N maps subsets of K into subsets of K, and K contains every primitive judgment.

Let S⁺ be the greatest fixed point of N on subsets of J, and S⁻ the greatest fixed point of its restriction to subsets of K.
Because N preserves K, S⁻ is also a fixed point of the unrestricted N; both therefore reconstruct the full candidate equations.
No claim about the least full-N model is required.

The universal renewal no-sink proof survives both optimistic projections: every forcing step uses Q-free raw ranges/mappings, QName identity, affiliation methods/block and mandatory ancestry through z:X.
Every such candidate closure still contains a structurally nonprimitive proper extension before exact B, fresh relative to immutable Γ and owed in the newly generated context.
That duty is never removed from F_gen, so a finite R derivation would have a terminal candidate with no retained generated nonprimitive child, contradicting the hereditary invariant over all candidates, not only the explicit recipe.
The original root is therefore in D and absent from S⁻.

For S⁺, take the exact Cᵢ family and the separate actual-X root, augmented by every primitive own-Q judgment in each complete consumed closure, including actual Γ.
Nonprimitive members have the proved successor candidates; primitive members use their exact same-context epsilon candidates, retaining all inherited fresh roots and their already included duties.
This set is postfixed for N, placing the original root in S⁺.
Each candidate remains finite; the infinite support set witnesses membership in S⁺ rather than supplying an admitted infinite schema.

#### Primitive conditions, finite positives and formal nonentailment

Both constructed greatest models obey the accepted conditional primitive ground.
If its complete same-context no-change closure has all other duties true in S, adjoining the primitive judgment j gives a postfixed set S ∪ {j}: its exact identity candidate now has every required own clause.
Since every primitive belongs to K, greatestness forces j into either S⁻ or S⁺.
The same argument handles a finite same-context primitive block whose external debts already hold: adjoining the block supplies each exact identity candidate's full frontier.
This proves a property of the two constructed models, rather than inferring a new block ground from mutually conditional rules or assigning own-Q truth when a consumed duty fails.

Every accepted complete finite certificate also has finite postfixed support P for N.
Collect every own-Q in each checked actual/hypothetical closure: supplied nonprimitive children provide their candidate witnesses, while primitive members use exact same-context epsilon witnesses.
All inherited fresh roots required by those epsilon candidates are already roots of that context's complete B-v1 closure; original typed dependencies retain their original contexts.

After projecting primitive and Γ-owned premise duties, every retained generated nonprimitive dependency follows the certificate's finite child DAG.
Each nonprimitive member of P, including each actual Γ target, has its supplied candidate and hence a finite R derivation through those retained children.
This argument does not assume that dependencies owned by Γ are acyclic; they remain in the full postfixed support while the auxiliary derivation ignores their back-support.
Hence P is disjoint from D and lies in K; postfixedness places it in both S⁻ and S⁺.
Legal primitive component cycles therefore retain their accepted finite positives without becoming construction-proof backedges or requiring a least-model interpretation.
Necessary-local-contradiction negatives, including the trapped exact-vacuous consumer, hold in both models.

Original Γ's complete assessment includes primitive B and nonprimitive X, as well as its independent source/local premises.
All these duties hold in S⁺; X fails in S⁻, so the complete actual-assessment conjunction differs without assuming any original Q during qualification.

The explicitly formalized B-v1 contract therefore entails neither the renewal root judgment nor its negation.
This is a model-theoretic nonentailment result, stronger than assigning false to the displayed chain implications alone.
It selects no semantic convention, proves no independent normative success or nonexistence, and establishes neither general undecidability nor impossibility of a complete procedure for an independently settled judgment.

#### Algorithm applicability and the next gate

The inspected algorithms address precise neighboring problems but supply no missing B-v1 semantic introduction rule.
A finite quotient still needs exact extraction/reconstruction and semantic soundness before any cyclic or automata procedure can decide the independent judgment.

| Primary research route | Applicable result | Missing B-v1 premise |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [FSA2003, Algorithm 3](https://www.cogsci.ed.ac.uk/~ht/XML_Europe_2003.html) | Proposes subsumption in place of case-based restriction | Accepted relation and contextual helper existence differ |
| [Cyclic descent](https://www.pure.ed.ac.uk/ws/portalfiles/portal/12304236/bs_journal.pdf), Definition 5.5/Proposition 5.8 | Sound cyclic induction with a global trace condition | Chosen inductive meaning and proved descending traces |
| [Coinductive equations](https://xavierleroy.org/publi/coindsem-journal.pdf), Sections 2/7 | Guarded proofs for a specified coinductive interpretation | Independent correspondence with intended Q |
| [Regular coinduction](https://lmcs.episciences.org/8539/pdf), Theorem 5.2/Proposition 6.3 | Finite circular evidence and separate specification soundness | Exact labels do not repeat; a sound quotient is unproved |
| [Nominal automata](https://lmcs.episciences.org/1157/pdf), Example 2.5/Theorem 5.2 | Orbit-finite quotients under name symmetry | Renaming preserves the growing number of retained records |
| [WSTS](https://www.cmi.ac.in/~madhavan/courses/concurrency2017/literature/finkel-schnoebelen-wsts.pdf), Definition 2.5/Theorem 3.6 | Coverability with compatible order/effective predecessor bases | Helper inclusion need not preserve success; effective bases unproved |

FSA2003's proposed relation change is also distinguished in the [documented case-based/subsumption comparison](https://lists.w3.org/Archives/Public/xmlschema-dev/2005Aug/0054.html); it is not an implementation of the selected particle rule.
For WSTS, adding the locally legal retained consumers to the exact-vacuous U/M parent destroys its complete child, so plain helper inclusion supplies no success-preserving order.
Arbitrary B-v1 candidate generation also has unbounded branching, and the existential-candidate/all-required-frontiers problem is not automatically the inspected single-player coverability problem.

The next concrete gate is an independently justified dated-semantic principle distinguishing these formal models, followed by its correspondence proof with the full B-v1 predicates before a general algorithm is implemented.
The finite two-step construction bound is not a bound on validation-proof depth, while legal recursive property closure does not itself justify coinduction across generated contexts.
General witness coverage, adequate proof representation, input-derived bounds or an equivalent complete-negative criterion and mathematical termination remain open.

All accepted packages, original operands, domain/evidence versions, failure categories and configurable sticky engineering budgets remain unchanged.
No new production rule, cyclic acceptance, helper quotient, policy choice or activation is introduced; #179/#153/#184 retain their separate gates.

### Executed source-to-B semantic bridge audit

This investigation executes the [prepared packet](content-model-s06-research-handoff.md#current-purpose-and-next-investigation) at main `603d84dad2e5942e2aa76ee7e119c1c98661b8a4`, tree `a924ba281368b54ed8e6b0c08f51e2bb259d4bcf`.
Its subjects are actual q_X = Q_B(Γ,Γ,X,A) and qᵢ = Q_B(Γ,Cᵢ,Uᵢ,A) from the consumed renewal source.
It tests the dated-source correspondence and concrete semantic inference attempts, rather than treating formal nonentailment as a verdict about intended normative meaning.

The independently reviewed result is a scoped proof obstacle: the inspected sources do not justify either attempted truth rule below, and no compatible omitted premise was established.
This is not an exhaustive absence theorem about all sources, a semantic answer to renewal, full-XSD ambiguity or undecidability.
The following paired control also disproves one optional history-erasing reduction; a direct history-aware proof need not establish that reduction.

The phrase requiring components to satisfy their constraints does not introduce a missing shortcut: restricting candidate quantification to fully V-qualified components is precisely the full frontier condition already present in N.
Conformance's prohibition on assessing an instance against an invalid schema does not forbid representing and rejecting proposed construction records.
Likewise, selected B supplies a finite H for each check; native property-reference closure is not a claim that the universe of possible constructions is finite.

The term-definition route is a real compatibility question, not language dismissed as a note.
Importing unrestricted instance-language inclusion would reject the already accepted [dead-intermediate contrasts](#source-authority-and-corrections), where numbered component restriction permits a nonempty restriction of an empty-language base.
No independently justified narrower part of that description was found that distinguishes renewal while preserving those controls.

#### Two concrete unselected principles and their failed inferences

Let T_B denote the audited formalized candidate equations and additional accepted primitive/finite-positive rules.
One proposed positive principle is semantic reflection:

```text
P_sat(j): Q(j) iff there exists an interpretation S satisfying T_B with j ∈ S.
```

This would make renewal positive through S⁺.
Its first unsupported step replaces existence of a finite component construction c satisfying V(c), with predicates already interpreted, by existence of an interpretation S and a construction satisfying V_S(c).
An interpretation of Q is neither one of the schema component kinds nor a component property supplied by the native construction vocabulary.

S⁻ is a concrete countermodel to deriving this reflection from T_B: existence of the compatible model S⁺ is true while renewal is false in S⁻.
This rejects the proposed inference from the audited premises; it does not prove that no other dated semantic source could justify an additional principle.
Calling a consistent postfixed family valid would make the same unsupported truth-introduction step.

The proposed necessary negative foundation is:

```text
P_found: for every nonprimitive j, Q(j) implies j ∈ μR.
```

R is exactly the optimistic auxiliary operator above, not the full candidate operator N and not semantic validity.
This principle would exclude renewal while preserving accepted finite positives and legal primitive component cycles; it is only a necessary condition, not a complete interpretation or decision procedure.
Its first unsupported implication is from wellfounded base ancestry and finite property closure inside each H to finite generated-context support across H₀,H₁,....

Every Uᵢ still has Uᵢ→B→A→anyType, so fixed base height coexists with the displayed unbounded contextual support.
S⁺ satisfies the audited structural/source premises but violates this foundation principle.
The two-step construction bound, conditional primitive approval and rejection of cyclic stored evidence do not supply the missing implication.

#### A paired anchor control and its exact projection

The new [independently authenticated source Γ′](../test/conformance/fixtures/xsd/recovery/exact-vacuous-consumer-reanchored.xsd) represents the current component properties of the earlier trapped parent P as a distinct actual input.
It retains A/B/R, declares X already restricting B to empty, U vacuously extending B, M:U affiliated to H, R_H restricting B with `[H1]` and R_M restricting R_H with `[M1]`.
Each request keeps its own immutable actual context throughout; no issued context is reset or reanchored.

Define π to retain the following semantic component-property graph and erase only source contributions, source positions, provenance and immutable-anchor/root labels.
The [typed comparison](../test/research/re01/exact-vacuous-ground.test.ts) constructs a bijection over both complete fifteen-member sets of represented canonical records: nine fixed global identities/QNames and six separately mapped particle incidences.
It preserves scoped particle roles/owners, distinct wrappers/uses, ordered edges, occurrence bounds, element references, base/method references and all sharing, including the authoritative effective A/B/U content identity within each input.
Sequence/model-group terms are carried inside wrapper particles; this record count is not a count of every native abstract component or builtin.

The comparison also preserves type/declaration flags, target namespaces, empty annotations/AUs, absent wildcards and value/identity constraints, and absent scalar slots.
It uses source-derived effective content/AUs where required, not equality of caller-supplied raw properties alone.
This is a specified projection, not full B-input isomorphism, preservation of source operands or an identity claim about property-equal clones.

Old P has all fifteen local premises qualified with authenticated correspondence, but is not fully valid: its mandatory U judgment is false by the preceding universal first-restriction/QName theorem.
Every U child must check inherited fresh R_M, which necessarily fails 5.4 under arbitrary permitted finite additions.
The executed rejected child illustrates that proof; it does not establish its universal quantifier.

New Γ′ has all fifteen actual local premises and a complete one-node U-restrict-B certificate.
At the PR315 revision, R_M remained checked in actual Γ′ and outside the ten-component hypothetical closure, while R_H was included and passed.
The [later identity-read repair](#identity-comparison-must-not-manufacture-validity-roots) removes that incidental R_H inclusion; both actual duties remain checked.
Checking R_M hypothetically with the authenticated step correspondence rejects 5.4, so the positive is not silently transferring its actual validity.

Consequently no function of π alone equals selected Q_B on both admitted locally qualified queries.
This does not refute factorization restricted to already fully valid parents or states reached by successful transformations, and does not describe two semantically valid schemas.
It establishes anchor sensitivity of selected B on this domain, not an implementation defect, same-input inconsistency or permission to change the accepted root policy.

Native factorization through π remains unproved: abstract component wording and same-assessment assembly invariance do not by themselves establish this cross-source correspondence.
If such native factorization and uniform selected-B/native correspondence were both proposed, this pair would prevent their conjunction on these queries.
A direct history-aware semantic proof need not erase Γ, prove cross-Γ invariance or transfer facts; its remaining dependency is the independently justified full-context truth rule described above.

The [independent source/reference control](../test/conformance/reference/re01-exact-vacuous-ground.test.ts) pins both consumer chains, exact named references, flags and source contributions, with live source/instance acceptance separate from RE01 authority.
The existing complete original-X and stable-M:B contrasts remain unchanged.
No recognizer, semantic owner, proof API or production success/negative rule changes.

#### Historical leads, read limits and concrete disposition

The [WG bug 2235](https://www.w3.org/Bugs/Public/show_bug.cgi?id=2235) resolution on 20 October 2006 changes the explanatory note for the next working draft rather than establishing a replacement dated normative rule.
The [24 October 2006 CMS mail](https://lists.w3.org/Archives/Public/xmlschema-dev/2006Oct/0072.html) conditions the two-step proposal on constructibility and rejects an intermediate violating EDC.
The coordinator read the full letter; the investigator inspected authenticated limited excerpts after full retrieval failed, and does not claim a complete thread audit.

The investigator read the bug resolution and current [second-edition errata page](https://www.w3.org/2004/03/xmlschema-errata); the listed Structures correction concerns default/PSVI behavior, with no adopted RE01 selector found there.
This is a scoped source inventory, not proof that no historical source or applicable correction exists.
Later-edition wording does not automatically amend the selected 2004 rule.

The coordinator inspected the [2001 Formal Description WD](https://www.w3.org/TR/xmlschema-formal/), Sections 5.3–5.4, status and Appendix B: its forest-subset relation and stated omissions do not establish equivalence with full accepted component derivation.
The [12 January 2003 notes](https://cmsmcq.com/2002/dctgnotes.html), Section 4, likewise leave component-constraint conformance as a proof obligation.
These qualified coordinator readings supply no missing full-B truth rule; substituting their relation would require a new correspondence theorem.
The whole XSTS archive, member-only discussions and unlisted later threads were not audited; no source-exhaustion conclusion is claimed.

| Unselected route | Renewal consequence | Preserved facts and exact proof cost |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Independently justified positive reflection or another introduction | Could establish semantic success beyond current finite evidence | Prove authority/correspondence and a sound adequate proof mechanism; retain primitive cycles and trapped-consumer negative |
| Independently justified generated-nonprimitive foundation | Would exclude renewal | Prove necessity from intended semantics; it preserves finite positives but supplies no complete positive criterion |
| Concrete missing premise or correspondence repair | Depends on the corrected relation | Identify the exact clause, repair both translation directions and re-evaluate the models and controls |
| Explicit project interpretation | Changes the stated contract only if separately selected | Specify alternatives and compatibility; a maintainer decision is not a source theorem |

The recommendation is to preserve B-v1 and every gate while recording this concrete proof dependency, rather than select a model by convenience or add a solver for an unsettled judgment.
No source-backed distinguishing truth rule or specific correspondence correction was established by this investigation after independent challenge.
This is a genuine scoped obstacle, not a negative inferred from the checkpoint, a search budget or sampled fixtures.

General witness coverage, semantic evidence adequacy, complete negatives and mathematical termination therefore remain open.
All original Γ obligations, exact owning contexts, inherited fresh roots, original typed operands, conditional primitive truth, legal primitive cycles, seven accepted packages and P1/F2 remain binding.
No policy is selected, no additional user approval is inferred, and #234/#232 or later #179/#153/#184 are not closed or activated.

### Founded contextual research completion and identity-read repair

This continuation starts at main `a606c8af846678523915c85468c2bfa599334207`, tree `d9e1f6e57dec1043bd4ea886f68510568af5594b`.
It investigates both earlier proposals and external recursive-validation methods, proves a conditional finite-forest result and repairs a concrete executable correspondence defect.
Under the user's delegated choice, the coordinator selects the additional research definition `founded-contextual-Q-v1` after independent compatibility review.

#### Exact selected relation and incompatible alternatives

Retain J, N, R, D and K exactly as defined in the [global formal-model proof](#global-formal-models-and-renewal-nonentailment), including every actual and hypothetical duty in full N/V.
The additional definition is:

```text
Q_f = greatest fixed point of N restricted to K = S⁻
K = J \ (Jₙ \ μR)
```

This is a separately named project research relation, not a theorem that dated XSD entails P_found or an adoption of ordinary logic-program/SHACL well-founded semantics.
It retains B-v1 geometry, immutable Γ, exact contexts and operands, all inherited fresh roots, conditional primitive truth, legal primitive component cycles and accepted finite positives.
It changes no domain export, finite-evidence authority, production policy/API, P1/F2 or accepted local package.
Faithfulness to the independent dated judgment remains a separate open obligation.

The two previous proposals cannot both be adopted unchanged.
For T_B, existence of a compatible model containing j is exactly j∈S⁺: every compatible model is an N fixed point contained in νN, and νN itself satisfies the accepted additional rules.
Thus P_sat selects S⁺, while P_found alone only requires Q⊆K and needs an additional completion to define a full relation.

The selected Q_f is the greatest such completion.
Renewal lies in S⁺ and outside μR, so P_sat makes it true while P_found excludes it.
S⁺ remains a model of the old T_B; it is not the selected new relation, and restricting P_sat to founded models would be a different proposal.

The recommendation favors the founded research route because it preserves the accepted positives and supports the finite-forest theorem below.
This is an explicit interpretation choice with stated consequences, not a source-derived answer hidden behind an algorithm.
It neither proves general negative decidability nor closes the dated-semantic correspondence gate.

#### Whole-assessment finite semantic forest theorem

Fix a finite admitted actual Γ and an applicable original RE01 target, with complete source, notation, scalar-slot, Q-free local and external-input qualifications.
Let A_Γ contain every actual nonprimitive own-Q obligation, not only the requested target.
Under the exact semantic closure conditions below:

```text
A_Γ ⊆ μR iff there is a complete finite semantic assessment forest for Γ.
Assessment_Q_f(Γ) iff there is such a forest.
```

The forest uses exact candidates and the existing primitive no-change discipline; it is distinct from arbitrary runtime acceptance or an input-derived search bound.
The required context-ownership condition is explicit: a candidate's full own-Q frontier belongs to its new H or immutable Γ.
Property/identity reads of old parent P do not assert full V(P), original typed operands retain Γ, and scalar-slot validity does not import the complex provenance owner's own clause.

For the forward direction, finitarity gives a finite R derivation for each member of the finite A_Γ.
Take their finite union; when one exact parent/target appears more than once, choose a minimum-height witness coherently and prune unused alternatives.
Every retained generated nonprimitive edge decreases that height, so the result is a finite DAG.

Each witness supplies one exact new context H, with all generated children owned by that H and every inherited fresh root retained.
Instantiate its context once and preserve its complete records, ordered incidences and bindings; distinct component identities are not quotient-merged.
Its L premises qualify independently of pending Q, and its generated nonprimitive duties have the lower-height witnesses.

Erased Γ-owned duties are discharged globally by the supplied forest for every actual nonprimitive and by actual primitive checking, not assumed true or inserted as construction backedges.
Primitive identity closures add no debt beyond the complete context frontier: their inherited fresh roots are already required, and their original typed/property dependencies stay in that closure.
Their finite same-context component blocks close under the accepted primitive discipline after the other duties are supplied, preserving legal property recursion without a new generated-cycle rule.

The reverse direction is the existing finite-positive extraction: project the forest's frontiers and use topological induction on its generated nonprimitive child DAG.
Each actual nonprimitive root then has a finite R derivation; no acyclicity of original Γ property references is assumed.

For the second equivalence, complete Q_f assessment puts all actual nonprimitive duties in S⁻⊆K, hence in μR, and the first implication supplies the forest.
Conversely, collect every own-Q in the semantic forest's complete actual/hypothetical closures.
Its nonprimitive witnesses and primitive identity closures give finite postfixed support for N; the projected child DAG puts every nonprimitive member in μR, hence the support lies in K.
Greatestness then places that support, including every actual duty and conditional primitive block, in S⁻ without assuming runtime materialization.
This proves whole-assessment adequacy for the selected relation under these semantic forest conditions, not adequacy for independent dated Q or for one isolated μR target with other actual debts missing.

Executable acceptance additionally requires authentic materialization of each chosen candidate/local plan and equality, or justified inclusion, between its semantic frontier and the verifier's scheduled frontier.
The accepted local rules must discharge every Q-free/scalar prerequisite in its own context.
These representation/frontier conditions remain explicit proof obligations beyond the concrete repair below; one regression fix does not establish arbitrary semantic-plan coverage.

#### Identity comparison must not manufacture validity roots

The new [source](../test/conformance/fixtures/xsd/recovery/exact-vacuous-identity-read.xsd) extends the reanchored control with empty A2, primitive T extending A2 with required Z, V restricting T with the same required Z and global Z:R_M.
All twenty-three represented actual records qualify locally, and U remains the sole actual nonprimitive obligation.
The U-restrict-B witness has no generated nonprimitive duty and does not consume unrelated original T, V, Z or R_M.

Before the repair, `checkCandidateCorrespondence.refKey` compared preserved original V.base=T and published T as a hypothetical type-validity dependency.
The finite verifier then closed T→Z→R_M and rejected R_M at 5.4, although T had only been read for exact identity preservation.
Independent authenticated probes reproduced this false negative before the edit.

The repair retains resolution, membership/role checks and collision-free identity comparison, while removing validity-debt publication from that pure comparison.
Genuine scalar/facet and ancestor dependencies continue to come from their existing owners; the finite verifier and all mandatory fresh roots remain unchanged.

| Generated scalars and notations | Original GSC1 slot membership, explicit fresh simple types and source-bound notation tables | Preserve scalar-slot versus complex-owner and original versus active operand contexts |

##### Authentic combined discriminator

The [independent source](../test/conformance/fixtures/xsd/recovery/original-property-projection-independent.xsd) has twenty-five represented actual records.
A/B/R/X are empty, A2 contains optional qualified p:string, H:A2 blocks restriction, and Left/Right own distinct anonymous C1/C2 directly extending A2.
Each scope adds optional qualified N:string followed by optional H, and a local a:string AU.
Each effective source content has its own private outer sequence over the shared A2 prefix and that scope's original tail; these generated origins are not actual members.
Γ has no global N, all actual source/local checks pass, and X is its sole nonprimitive duty.

The parent adds eleven real records, including direct-extension T of A2, global N:T affiliated to H, and FA/FR/FT with two anonymous declarations borrowing the C-scope AUs.
The complete construction inventory checks T's full conditional primitive block; FT is the only generated nonprimitive duty.
The identity child adds only FE and its unit outer particle and completes a two-node whole-input forest, checking all original and inherited fresh roots.
Neither C1 nor C2 is genuinely consumed in that hypothetical inventory.

Before this repair, the copied child without C rows stopped at `component-complex-properties`.
Faithfully supplying C rows and real outer sequences made recursive equality true and FE locally checked, but both mandatory outer sequences rejected EDC.
Fresh N:T remains effective under H's restriction block; each outer therefore includes QName-equal local N:string and implicit N:T with incompatible exact types.
A genuinely consumed C with those rows still rejects EDC.
The repair must support the sparse property read without exempting any installed wrapper or transferring a validity receipt from a different context.

The primary [pinned Structures source](#source-authority-and-corrections) supplies the independent facts: `e-props-correct.4` (lines 2838–2842) requires the affiliation's exact type ancestry; `cos-equiv-derived-ok-rec`/`cos-equiv-class` (2892–2958) retain the extension-only member; `cos-element-consistent` (5011–5037) applies to direct/implicit QName-equal declarations; `cos-nonambig` (5039–5058) distinguishes the original disjoint labels; `cos-particle-extend` (5293–5308) compares prefix properties rather than making an unconsumed scope a validity root.

##### Positive preservation argument

##### Exact fixed-prefix and root-range UPA bounds

Use the accepted nontrimmed reachable-prefix attribution semantics, with a fixed context/census and distinct incidence tags.
For unit concatenation P;S, let Exit(P) mean that some P run completes.
Let Bdry(P) collect labels enabled in a reachable P configuration set that also permits P exit after the same input prefix.
Let First(S) be the labels enabled in S's initial epsilon closure.

If P cannot exit, S is unreachable and concatenation UPA equals P's UPA.
Otherwise concatenation UPA is equivalent to P and S being UPA and `Bdry(P) ∩ First(S)` being empty.
For sufficiency, choose the earliest differing tags of two conflicting runs.
A conflict inside either factor contradicts its UPA; different entry offsets into S first produce a P/S boundary conflict.
This argument retains dead branches and does not require a completing word.

For a standalone fixed body G, its root minimum gates only final exit and cannot enable another consuming transition.
Its intrinsic UPA is therefore independent of that minimum and downward monotone in its positive root maximum.
The same conclusion applies to the final S of the stated unit concatenation, with its separate fixed-prefix boundary test.
It does not justify changing an inner occurrence range with a consuming continuation.

There is an effective finite maximum threshold for this fixed body.
Construct G with root range `0..unbounded` and let M bound its individual control/counter configurations, retaining inactive values and saturating each unbounded counter at its fixed minimum.
In the product of two configurations, allow epsilon steps in either run and paired consuming steps with an overlapping label.
A reachable pair with differently tagged overlapping next transitions is exactly a UPA conflict.
A simple path has at most `M*M-1` edges, so root maximum `K=M*M+1` admits its completed iterations and conflicting next transitions.

Thus either the unbounded root is conflict-free for every legal positive maximum, or some first bad finite maximum occurs among `1..K`.
Testing those finite cases yields the exact intrinsic threshold; test minimum zero and then use minimum independence only for legal intervals.
This is a mathematical finite-state bound, not permission to ignore shared engineering limits or interpret resource exhaustion as a negative.

For a supplied finite template library with one variable first-born S root interval, fixed internal ranges/census/source data and the stated isolated UPA placements, restriction normalization and mappings have finitely many structural cases.
Their arithmetic uses only addition and constant multiplication, including MapAndSum's fixed member count; zero, unit, infinity, all-placement and formal-emptiability cases remain explicit.
Classical Presburger decision followed by model enumeration gives a total numeric/digit bound for this supplied sector.
An inherited S retains its exact parent interval instead of being resynthesized.
The finite library and any forest shape are extra inputs: successful model-specific frontier coverage remains a separate premise, so this is not b(Γ,P,target).

Its formal effective total range is L, its live First set is empty, and it contributes no element declaration or EDC QName.

##### Incidences, dead syntax and inner exact counts

Use the accepted untrimmed reachable-prefix semantics: different AST child incidences have distinct tags,
while repeated activations of one incidence reuse its tag. Each tag has its exact fixed matching set.
No declaration-name/profile quotient identifies two positions, and no fact changes assessment context.
The grammar here is sequence, choice and counted repetition; all/interleaving is a separate case.

For fixed labels, lowering one positive exact count n to m, with `1<=m<=n`, preserves UPA in every
unchanged surrounding sequence/choice/count expression, including productive repeats and dead branches.
This closes the earlier local inner-count question; it does not preserve arbitrary arithmetic premises.
The productive step follows the inspected [Groz–Maneth algorithm](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jcss.2017.05.013),
section 3.4: exact lowering preserves returned flexibility values and can only remove flexible loop edges.

The imported predicate is global marked-word one-unambiguity, specified in Definition 2.1 and Theorem 6.6
of [Kilpeläinen–Tuhkanen's author preprint](https://www.cs.uku.fi/tutkimus/publications/reports/A-2006-2.pdf),
printed pages 4 and 20; Groz–Maneth Proposition 3.12 explicitly imports that result.
On productive syntax, every reachable consuming prefix completes; the earliest different tags of two
histories therefore give the same marked prefix and conflicting next positions in that definition.
Conversely such marked accepted words supply the two native histories. Their counter valuations need not agree.

The later paper's page-13 wording about one configuration is not used as an equivalent definition.
Its own Example 3.13, `(b? a2..3)3..3 b`, has a conflict after six a tokens across histories with
outer counters two and three; the power-two contrast passes. The semantic theorem accounts for those histories.
The primary semantic definition and characterization were inspected; Appendix C's full proof was not rederived.

For a productive root containing a maximal dead subtree H, first replace a zero-minimum activation of
a dead body by epsilon-or-body. H is then an alternative of a productive choice with a completing sibling.
Replace H by a choice of its ORIGINAL first positions p, each followed by its own private delimiter.
No old position is copied. Every completed p/delimiter block before a first conflict can be replaced,
in each run separately, by the same completing sibling word, preserving that run's outside counters.
Thus original UPA implies summary UPA; summary UPA plus intrinsic UPA of every hole implies original UPA.

Induct first on dead roots and then productive roots with the same dead-node count.
A dead left sequence factor prevents reaching its right factor; a positive repetition of a dead body
cannot return from its first activation. Productive summaries use the primary exact-lowering theorem.
Appending one fresh labelled probe gives the required boundary-label inclusion for the next induction step.
Fixed set labels reduce by choosing one representative per tag at the first conflict, without clones.
The argument proves a fixed-label attribution theorem, not source/property or complete-frontier lifting.

For disjoint-tag unit concatenation EF, V=V(E) and(not X(E) or(V(F) and D(E) disjoint F(F))).

Each descriptor has a selected positive match to a B_i leaf; intersect those fixed leaf ranges to l..u.
For t occurrences, aggregate minima M and finite maxima R have exact linear realizability constraints:

| Mode | Aggregate conditions for t>0 |
| --- | --- |
| Positive exact | M=R, M>=t*max(1,l), M<=t*u when u finite |
| Positive flexible finite | M>=t*max(1,l), R>=M+t, R<=t*u when u finite |
| Zero-min finite | l=0, M=0, R>=t, R<=t*u when u finite |
| Infinite maximum | u infinite; M=0 or M>=t*max(1,l), according to mode |

The t=0 case has zero aggregates. All products have fixed constant factors.

within capacities. This reconstructs individual intervals for EVERY aggregate model and preserves its mode.

accepting end, impose flow balances and connected nonzero undirected support containing the start.
Conversely the resulting directed multigraph has an Euler trail; reading it reconstructs an accepted word.

into private copies outside R. Keep all R-to-R selectors, including current-E self selectors
and cycles: an original endpoint can legitimately have a positive restriction-only path to E.
Every erased outside-R copy must still satisfy the complete forest-wide privacy premises.

If r is the supplied number of R copies, this leaves at most r semantic copy types and at most
r times the maximum finite Γ-template particle incidence size, apart from fixed P/source sharing
and owner support. This removes dependence on recursive cover depth. It derives neither r nor
owner support from Γ/P. Bounded keys per query and finitely many original retirements per branch
do not alone bound all protected roles across contexts or reconstruct arbitrary fresh consumers.

The retained-role control uses the actual original D intermediate t0 in 5.4. It retains t0 and
